## Confirmed

- **C-0005** — code: A block restricts resource usage in a driving course to either a single vehicle at a time (SINGLE_VEHICLE_ONLY) or to multiple vehicles entering in the same direction (SAME_DIRECTION_ONLY). ([`Block.java:209-214`](https://github.com/alxp1982/opentcs/blob/742b0e6df9ccbabc660e585a85e55d585c0642fc/opentcs-api-base/src/main/java/org/opentcs/data/model/Block.java#L209-L214))  
  doc ([`plant-model.yaml:1352`](https://github.com/alxp1982/opentcs/blob/742b0e6df9ccbabc660e585a85e55d585c0642fc/opentcs-documentation/src/docs/service-web-api-v1/paths/plant-model.yaml#L1352)): plant-model:PlantModelBlock.type is one of: SINGLE_VEHICLE_ONLY, SAME_DIRECTION_ONLY. This block's type.  
  _Both the plant model Block type schema (D-1200, D-2223) and reference documentation (D-0449, D-0156) confirm that a block type is restricted to SINGLE_VEHICLE_ONLY or SAME_DIRECTION_ONLY._
- **C-0008** — code: A drive order and its destination state progress through PRISTINE (unprocessed), TRAVELLING (moving toward destination), OPERATING (executing destination operation), and conclude with either FINISHED (successful completion) or FAILED. ([`DriveOrder.java:352-368`](https://github.com/alxp1982/opentcs/blob/742b0e6df9ccbabc660e585a85e55d585c0642fc/opentcs-api-base/src/main/java/org/opentcs/data/order/DriveOrder.java#L352-L368))  
  doc ([`transport-order.yaml:171`](https://github.com/alxp1982/opentcs/blob/742b0e6df9ccbabc660e585a85e55d585c0642fc/opentcs-documentation/src/docs/service-web-api-v8/schemas/objects-states/transport-order.yaml#L171)): transport-order:DriveOrderState is one of: PRISTINE, TRAVELLING, OPERATING, FINISHED, FAILED.  
  _DriveOrderState enum values PRISTINE, TRAVELLING, OPERATING, FINISHED, and FAILED match the lifecycle progression in D-2392, D-1369, and D-1497._
- **C-0009** — code: An environmental entity's integration level indicates whether it is ignored (TO_BE_IGNORED), displayed without impacting traffic management (TO_BE_NOTICED), or taken into account by traffic management (TO_BE_RESPECTED). ([`EnvironmentalEntity.java:462-470`](https://github.com/alxp1982/opentcs/blob/742b0e6df9ccbabc660e585a85e55d585c0642fc/opentcs-api-base/src/main/java/org/opentcs/data/model/EnvironmentalEntity.java#L462-L470))

The entity's integration level.  
  _Environmental entity integration levels TO_BE_IGNORED, TO_BE_NOTICED, and TO_BE_RESPECTED agree with OpenAPI schemas D-0870, D-1752, and D-2260 as well as the User's Guide (D-0498, D-0499)._
